<h3 id="350">[350] Why Do Safety Guardrails Degrade Across Languages?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17173](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17173) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Max Zhang, Ameen Patel, Sang T. Truong, Sanmi Koyejo
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Multilingual Learning, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文引入多组项目反应理论框架来解耦LLM安全退化的因素，发现安全主要是单维度的，低资源语言产生更多不确定响应，严重的翻译错误和文化概念不匹配会导致跨语言安全差距。

<h3 id="351">[351] Beyond Execution: Static-Analysis Rewards and Hint-Conditioned Diffusion RL for Code Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17174](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17174) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shuyin Ouyang, Zhaozhi Qian, Faroq AL-Tam, Muhammad AL-Qurishi, Jie M. Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Reinforcement Learning, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 本文研究了扩散语言模型代码生成的强化学习后训练，发现静态检查奖励和提示条件采样可以改善性能，特别是在执行奖励较低的困难任务上效果更显著。

<h3 id="352">[352] MusicSynth: An Automated Pipeline for Generating Violin Fingerboard Animations from Sheet Music Using Optical Music Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17181](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17181) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abhimanyu Kaushik
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Music Generation, OCR, Education Technology
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了MusicSynth，一个开源网络工具，利用光学音乐识别技术自动从乐谱生成小提琴指板动画，在清晰印刷音乐上达到91.2%的音符识别准确率。

<h3 id="353">[353] PluRule: A Benchmark for Moderating Pluralistic Communities on Social Media</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17187](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17187) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zoher Kachwala, Bao Tran Truong, Rasika Muralidharan, Haewoon Kwak, Jisun An, Filippo Menczer
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Content Moderation, Benchmark, Vision-Language Model
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了PluRule，一个用于检测社交媒体多元化社区违规行为的多模态、多语言基准测试，涵盖1989个Reddit社区的2885条规则。研究表明，即使是最先进的视觉语言模型在此任务上也表现不佳，揭示了AI模型在多元化社区内容审核方面面临根本性挑战。

<h3 id="354">[354] Event-Grounded Sparse Autoencoders for Vision-Language-Action Policies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17204](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17204) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinchen Jin, Aditya Chatterjee, Pranav Kumar, Rohan Paleja
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Robotics, Vision-Language Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/xc-j/Event-SAE)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种事件锚定的可解释性分析流程，将稀疏自编码器(SAE)特征分析与行为事件而非文本上下文关联，用于视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略模型。该方法在OpenVLA上产生了最强的因果效应，并为行为锚定的VLA可解释性提供了实用起点。

<h3 id="355">[355] Integration of AI in Cybersecurity: Current Trends with a Focused Look at Intrusion Detection Applications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17219](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17219) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: S. Tazili, A. Mansour, M. Y. Chkouri
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.NI; eess.SP
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, Intrusion Detection, Survey
- **Venue**: AI2SD 2025
- **Summary**: 本文综述了当前AI在网络安全领域的应用趋势，重点关注入侵检测方法。作者分析了生成式AI、自然语言处理、联邦学习和可解释AI等技术，并基于所采用的AI技术和报告的性能进行了比较分析。

<h3 id="356">[356] Systematic Evaluation of Vision Transformers for Automated Cervical Cancer Classification: Optimization, Statistical Validation, and Clinical Interpretability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17236](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17236) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nisreen Albzour, Sarah S. Lam
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Vision Transformer, Cancer Diagnosis
- **Summary**: 本文系统优化了视觉Transformer架构用于自动化宫颈癌筛查，在Herlev数据集上实现了94.9%-95.2%的交叉验证准确率。Grad-CAM分析证实模型注意力与临床相关的形态学特征相对应，满足医疗AI部署所需的临床性能和透明度要求。

<h3 id="357">[357] Drift Flow Matching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17244](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17244) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chenrui Ma, Xi Xiao, Lin Zhao, Tianyang Wang, Ferdinando Fioretto, Yanning Shen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Flow Matching, Generative Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Drift Flow Matching (DFM)框架，将一步生成模型与基于流的迭代生成方法连接起来。DFM在保持直接传输映射效率的同时，允许通过多次推理步骤优化生成质量，提供了可根据不同质量-效率需求调整采样计算的新生成范式。

<h3 id="358">[358] Fidelity Probes for Specification--Code Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17246](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17246) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ferhat Erata, Hao Zhou, Luke Huan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Software Documentation, Program Verification, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文引入了保真度探针(fidelity probes)，即从代码生成的自然语言问题，用于测量规范与代码之间的一致性。作者在COBOL基准测试上将规范保真度从0.63提升至0.94，展示了LLM和符号分析方法的互补性。

<h3 id="359">[359] Latency-Aware Deep Learning Benchmark for Real-Time Cyber-Physical Attack and Fault Classification in Inverter-Dominated Power Grids</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17256](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17256) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Emad Abukhousa, Saman Zonouz, A.P. Sakis Meliopoulos
- **Subjects**: eess.SY; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Power System, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文引入了一个延迟感知的基准测试框架，用于评估电力系统异常检测中的深度学习模型。研究发现虽然分类决策在一个周期内完成，但端到端推理延迟始终超过三个周期，揭示了算法能力与保护级部署之间的关键差距。

<h3 id="360">[360] State-of-the-Art Claims Require State-of-the-Art Evidence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17273](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17273) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: YongKyung Oh
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Model Evaluation, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: 本文识别了AI基准测试中普遍存在的声明-证据差距问题，指出SOTA声明往往缺乏充分的证据支持。作者分析了十个跨领域基准测试，发现超过一半的顶级模型比较中，优越性的常见假设属性并不成立，呼吁更诚实地报告结果。

<h3 id="361">[361] How Do Electrocardiogram Models Scale?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17276](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17276) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiawei Li, Fabio Bonassi, Ming Jin, Stefan Gustafsson, Johan Sundström, Thomas B. Schön, Antônio H. Ribeiro
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Scaling Laws, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: 本文系统研究了心电图（ECG）模型的扩展定律，比较了不同架构和预训练范式在模型规模和数据规模变化下的表现。研究发现自监督学习模型在扩展性上优于监督学习，且ResNet在参数效率上优于Transformer，为构建有效的ECG基础模型提供了指导。

<h3 id="362">[362] Rover: Context-aware Conflict Resolution with LLM</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17279](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17279) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qingyu Zhang, Junzhe Li, Jiayi Lin, Changhua Luo, Chenxiong Qian
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: ECG Analysis, Scaling Laws, Foundation Model, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: 本文系统研究了ECG模型的缩放定律，通过在CODE数据集上预训练超过120个模型，解耦了模型架构和预训练范式的影响。研究发现自监督学习模型在模型和数据规模上都能稳健扩展，ResNet在分布外泛化方面比Transformer更具参数效率。

<h3 id="363">[363] ContractBench: Can LLM Agents Preserve Observation Contracts?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17281](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17281) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Merging, Software Engineering, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了Rover，一个将程序分析与大语言模型集成的代码合并冲突解决系统。作者提出了多层代码属性图来捕获文件间依赖关系，证明Rover在字符、词法和语义层面上都优于现有方法。

<h3 id="364">[364] OProver: A Unified Framework for Agentic Formal Theorem Proving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17283](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17283) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="365">[365] CLAP: Contrastive Latent-space Prompt Optimization for End-to-end Autonomous Driving</h3>

<h3 id="366">[366] UNR-Explainer: Counterfactual Explanations for Unsupervised Node Representation Learning Models</h3>

<h3 id="367">[367] When Efficiency Backfires: Cascading LLMs Trigger Cascade Failure under Adversarial Attack</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17288](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17288) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文首次研究了LLM级联系统对针对性对抗攻击的脆弱性。作者提出了一种新的攻击框架，通过约束序列协作优化对抗后缀，同时利用轻量级模型和决策机制，实现对成本效率和准确性的可控降级。

<h3 id="368">[368] LEAP: Learnable End-to-End Adaptive Pruning of Large Language Models</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文介绍了LEAP，一种端到端非结构化掩码学习方法，用于LLM剪枝，采用逐权重Bernoulli-via-Gumbel-sigmoid松弛。在五个LLM家族上，LEAP在六任务平均零样本准确率上比最佳分层基线提高2.59个百分点。

<h3 id="369">[369] ConflictRAG: Detecting and Resolving Knowledge Conflicts in Retrieval Augmented Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17301](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17301) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="370">[370] Deep Reinforcement Learning Framework for Diversified Portfolio Management Across Global Equity Markets</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17307](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17307) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: q-fin.PM; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.NE; q-fin.TR
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Quantitative Finance, Portfolio Optimization
- **Summary**: 本研究开发了一个深度强化学习框架，使用Soft Actor-Critic算法进行全球股票市场的动态投资组合配置。框架将交易成本、换手率惩罚和分散化约束纳入奖励函数，在欧洲市场表现出统计显著的异常收益，但在所有市场未能持续超越买入持有策略。

<h3 id="371">[371] StyleText: A Large-Scale Dataset and Benchmark for Stylized Scene Text Inpainting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17309](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17309) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 本文提出了StyleText，一个用于风格保持的场景文本修复的大规模数据集和基准。数据集包含28,518个图像-掩码-提示三元组，采用自动化流水线构建，并定义了基于OCR指标和CLIP相似度的可复现评估协议。

<h3 id="372">[372] Attention Hijacking: Response Manipulation Across Queries in Vision-Language Models</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了Attention Hijacking攻击方法，通过操纵视觉语言模型的注意力分布来实现跨查询的响应操控。该方法通过增强视觉标记对目标响应的影响并抑制文本标记的竞争影响，显著提高了攻击在不同查询间的可迁移性。

<h3 id="373">[373] Weak-to-Strong Elicitation via Mismatched Wrong Drafts</h3>

- **Authors**: Wei Deng
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Mathematical Reasoning, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 本文研究了在强化学习微调中注入来自较小模型的错误草稿如何激发更强模型的能力。实验表明，将错误草稿与问题错配后注入GRPO上下文，在MATH-500和AIME基准上持续优于标准GRPO，在Mathstral-7B上达到71.98%的MATH-500准确率。

<h3 id="374">[374] Learning Higher-Order Structure from Incomplete Spatiotemporal Data: Multi-Scale Hypergraph Laplacians with Neural Refinement</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Time Series Analysis, Data Imputation
- **Summary**: 本文提出了多尺度超图拉普拉斯算子(MSHL)框架，用于从不完整的时空观测中学习高阶结构。该方法结合拓扑和残差相关性证据构建多尺度超图，并添加超图条件残差网络进行非线性修正，在交通网络的多种缺失场景下表现优异。

<h3 id="375">[375] TClone: Low-Latency Forking of Live GUI Environments for Computer-Use Agents</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.OS; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, GUI Automation, System Design
- **Summary**: 本文提出了TClone，一个面向计算机使用代理的可分叉个人工作空间系统。该系统支持实时GUI工作空间的快照、分支、回滚和选择性提交，通过兄弟容器、写时复制内存共享等技术，相比KVM和CRIU分别降低1.9倍和1.5倍的任务延迟。

<h3 id="376">[376] ASPI: Seeking Ambiguity Clarification Amplifies Prompt Injection Vulnerability in LLM Agents</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Prompt Injection, LLM Security, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/scaleapi/aspi)
- **Summary**: 本文引入ASPI基准，研究澄清行为如何放大LLM代理对提示注入攻击的脆弱性。实验表明，澄清状态下的攻击成功率显著上升，如o3从1.8%升至34.0%，揭示了标准执行时安全评估系统性地低估了交互式代理的攻击面。

<h3 id="377">[377] Efficient Feature-Free Initialization for Monocular Visual-Inertial Systems Using a Feed-Forward 3D Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17327](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17327) (cross-listed)

- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Yuantai-Z/FF-VIO-Init)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种无特征初始化框架，利用前馈3D模型预测的点云进行单目视觉惯性导航系统初始化。该方法消除了对视觉特征跟踪的依赖，在公共数据集上实现超过90%的成功率，并将初始化所需的数据时长缩短至1.2秒以下。

<h3 id="378">[378] LPG: Balancing Efficiency and Policy Reasoning in Latent Policy Guardrails</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17329](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17329) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI

- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/SaFo-Lab/Latent_Policy_Guard)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了潜在策略护栏(LPG)框架，用于动态安全策略执行。LPG将意图解释和策略锚定的内部推理压缩为连续状态，在保持审计能力的同时避免显式推理的延迟，LPG-4B达到84.5%的平均安全准确率，运行速度比Qwen3-4B-Thinking快约11倍。

<h3 id="379">[379] Single-Sample Black-Box Membership Inference Attack against Vision-Language Models via Cross-modal Semantic Alignment</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种针对视觉语言模型的单样本黑盒成员推理攻击框架。该方法利用跨模态语义对齐来量化图像-标题对齐程度，在严格的黑盒和单样本设置下，对LLaVA-1.5达到0.821的AUC，显著优于现有基线。

<h3 id="380">[380] Transitivity Meets Cyclicity: Explicit Preference Decomposition for Dynamic Large Language Model Alignment</h3>

- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/lab-klc/Hybrid-Reward-Cyclic)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了混合奖励-循环(HRC)模型，利用博弈论分解将偏好显式解耦为传递性和循环性分量。结合动态自博弈偏好优化(DSPPO)，该方法在RewardBench 2和AlpacaEval 2.0等基准上持续优于BT和GPM基线。

<h3 id="381">[381] \textsc{MasFACT}: Continual Multi-Agent Topology Learning via Geometry-Aware Posterior Transfer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17361](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17361) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xuefei Wang, Jialu Wang, Fengbo Zhang, Yihan Hu, Di Zhang, Yutong Ye, Yikun Ban, Jun Han, Ruijie Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Continual Learning, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 本文提出了MasFACT框架，用于持续多智能体拓扑学习，解决拓扑遗忘问题。该方法通过Fused Gromov-Wasserstein最优传输在任务特定的智能体空间间传递先验，并执行PAC-Bayes引导的保守后验适应，在类、域和任务级持续设置中表现优异。

<h3 id="382">[382] FML-bench: A Controlled Study of AI Research Agent Strategies from the Perspective of Search Dynamics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17373](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17373) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qiran Zou, Hou Hei Lam, Wenhao Zhao, Tingting Chen, Yiming Tang, Samson Yu, Yingtao Zhu, Srinivas Anumasa, Zufeng Zhang, Tianyi Zhang, Chang Liu, Zhengyao Jiang, Anirudh Goyal, Dianbo Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, LLM Agent, AutoML
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/qrzou/FML-bench)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了FML-Bench基准，包含18个基础ML研究任务，用于评估AI研究代理策略。基准将代理策略与执行基础设施分离，定义了12个过程级行为指标，发现简单贪婪爬山策略几乎匹配最佳树搜索代理的表现。

<h3 id="383">[383] Learning Faster with Better Tokens: Parameter-Efficient Vocabulary Adaptation for Specialized Text Summarization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17379](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17379) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gunjan Balde, Soumyadeep Roy, Mainack Mondal, Niloy Ganguly
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Summarization, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, Tokenization
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/gb-kgp/VocabReplace-Then-Expand)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种参数高效的词汇适应方法，用于专业领域文本摘要。该方法将领域特定标记添加到预训练分词器中，同时选择性替换训练不足的标记，在法律和医疗摘要任务上提高了语义相似度，并将训练时间减少35-55%。

<h3 id="384">[384] Ablating Safety: Mechanisms for Removing Alignment in Language Models for Security Applications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17413](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17413) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Isaac David, Arthur Gervais
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, LLM Security, Cybersecurity
- **Summary**: 本文研究了语言模型中对齐移除技术用于授权安全任务。评估了拒绝方向投影、表示控制投影和LoRA去对齐等方法，发现任务专用LoRA在保持通用能力的同时将安全分数提升至0.87，支持将对齐移除作为效用-风险前沿进行评估。

<h3 id="385">[385] IVF-TQ: Streaming-Robust Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search via a Codebook-Free Residual Layer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17415](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17415) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tarun Sharma
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DB; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Vector Search, Approximate Nearest Neighbor, Information Retrieval
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/tarun-ks/turboquant_search)
- **Summary**: 本文提出IVF-TQ，一种带有无码本残差层的近似最近邻搜索索引，通过固定随机旋转和预计算标量量化来解决流式场景下的索引陈旧问题。实验表明，在流式数据下IVF-TQ能保持稳定的召回率，而传统PQ方法显著下降。

<h3 id="386">[386] Benchmarking Mythos-Linked Bug Rediscovery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17416](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17416) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Isaac David, Arthur Gervais
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vulnerability Detection, LLM Evaluation, Software Testing
- **Summary**: 本文评估了三个大语言模型在六项与Anthropic Mythos相关的系统漏洞发现任务上的表现。实验显示GPT-5.5仅达成5/18的目标重新发现，模型的主要失败模式是过早锁定看似合理的替代候选而错过真正的漏洞根因。

<h3 id="387">[387] Learning Displacement-Robust Representations for Landslide Early Warning under Rainfall Forecast Uncertainty</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17419](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17419) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ren Ozeki, Hamada Rizk, Hirozumi Yamaguchi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Disaster Response, Contrastive Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出一种对降雨场位移鲁棒的滑坡预警系统，通过降雨运动感知对比学习(RMCL)从降雨和地形数据中学习在位移下保持稳定的潜在表示。该方法在日本19个地区的实验中比最先进基线精度提高达37%。

<h3 id="388">[388] Progressive Generalization Augmentation with Deeply Coupled RND-PPO and Domain-Prioritized Noise Injection for Robust Crop Management Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17428](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17428) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wu Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Agricultural AI, Robustness Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文针对农业强化学习系统的鲁棒性问题，提出渐进式泛化增强(PGA)、深度耦合RND-PPO架构和领域优先噪声注入三种创新。实验显示该方法在产量和氮利用效率上显著提升，并在扰动下保持94.4%的性能保留率。

<h3 id="389">[389] MATE: Solving Contextual Markov Decision Processes with Memory of Accumulated Transition Embeddings</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17431](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17431) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Himchan Hwang, Hyeokju Jeong, Gene Chung, Seungyeon Kim, Sangwoong Yoon, Frank Chongwoo Park
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Memory Architecture, Reinforcement Learning, Decision Making
- **Summary**: 本文提出MATE，一种用于求解上下文马尔可夫决策过程的简单有效记忆架构。MATE用求和聚合记忆替代不可计算的后验信念，避免了Transformer的增长计算成本和RNN的梯度问题，同时达到可比的性能。

<h3 id="390">[390] DiagEval: Trajectory-Conditioned Diagnosis for Reliable Software Evaluation with GUI Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17439](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17439) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sirui Hong, Zhijie Liu, Tengfei Li, Wei Tao, Yifan Wu, Chenglin Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: GUI Automation, LLM Evaluation, Software Testing
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/scutGit/DiagEval)
- **Summary**: 本文提出DiagEval，一种用于GUI代理评估交互软件的轨迹条件诊断评估协议。该方法重用失败轨迹进行针对性诊断探测，在假阴性案例中恢复了45.6-62.1%最初被错误归因于软件缺陷的失败。

<h3 id="391">[391] Beyond Catalogue Counts: the Dataset Visibility Asymmetry in Low-Resource Multilingual NLP</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17442](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17442) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhiyin Tan, Changxu Duan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Low-Resource NLP, Linguistic Resource, Data Quality
- **Venue**: LREC 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/zhiyintan/dataset-visibility-asymmetry)
- **Summary**: 本文研究了低资源语言数据集编目记录与研究文献中实际数据集流通之间的差距。通过LLM辅助的引用挖掘，作者在53种语言中识别出609个独特数据集，揭示了显著的可见性差距，表明多语言数据稀缺不仅是生产问题，也是文档化和可发现性问题。

<h3 id="392">[392] MemRepair: Hierarchical Memory for Agentic Repository-Level Vulnerability Repair</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17444](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17444) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Simiao Liu, Li Zhang, Fang Liu, Xiaoli Lian, Yang Liu, Yinghao Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Vulnerability Detection, LLM Agent, Program Repair
- **Summary**: 本文提出MemRepair，一个记忆增强的代理框架，用于仓库级漏洞修复，结合三层记忆和动态反馈驱动的精炼循环。该系统通过持久化学习先前的修复和失败尝试，在多个基准上达到最先进的解决率。

<h3 id="393">[393] Spatial Blindness in Whole-Slide Multiple Instance Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17449](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17449) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiangyu Li, Ran Su
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Multiple Instance Learning, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 本文识别出全切片多实例学习模型中的"空间盲区"问题，即模型的预测在补丁坐标被置换后几乎不变。作者提出ResTopoMIL，通过先拟合置换不变的原型直方图，再冻结它让轻量图分支在坐标打乱约束下学习残差来解决此问题。

<h3 id="394">[394] ContraFix: Agentic Vulnerability Repair via Differential Runtime Evidence and Skill Reuse</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17450](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17450) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Simiao Liu, Fang Liu, Li Zhang, Yang Liu, Yinghao Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Vulnerability Detection, LLM Agent, Program Repair
- **Summary**: 本文提出ContraFix，一个结合差分运行时证据和可复用修复技能的代理漏洞修复框架。系统构建跨越失败边界的PoC变体，分析崩溃与非崩溃执行之间的状态分歧，并维护双轨技能库供未来实例检索使用。

<h3 id="395">[395] GCE-MIL: Faithful and Recoverable Evidence for Multiple Instance Learning in Whole-Slide Imaging</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17456](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17456) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiangyu Li, Ran Su
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Multiple Instance Learning, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 本文提出GCE-MIL，一个骨干无关的多实例学习包装器，通过充分的充分性、必要性和可恢复性准则直接优化证据质量。该方法提高了分类和生存预测性能，同时减少了连续注意力与离散补丁子集之间的差距。

<h3 id="396">[396] Artificial Intelligence can Recognize Whether a Job Applicant is Selling and/or Lying According to Facial Expressions and Head Movements Much More Correctly Than Human Interviewers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17461](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17461) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hung-Yue Suen, Kuo-En Hung, Che-Wei Liu, Yu-Sheng Su, Han-Chih Fan
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Affective Computing, Computer Vision, Behavioral Modeling
- **Summary**: 本文开发深度学习模型，从视频面试中的面部表情和头部运动中识别诚实和欺骗性的印象管理策略。模型分别解释了91%和84%的诚实和欺骗性印象管理方差，表现优于人类面试官。

<h3 id="397">[397] An Interpretable Closed-Loop Intelligent Tutoring System for Multimodal Affective Feedback in Asynchronous Presentation Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17468](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17468) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hung-Yue Suen, Kuo-En Hung
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Education Technology, Multimodal Learning, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 本文提出一个可解释的闭环智能辅导系统，用于异步演讲训练，采用三层可解释反馈架构连接多模态评分、观众感知表达诊断和检索增强对话辅导。系统在七维行为锚定评分量表上达到与专家评分相当的性能，学习者表现出显著提升。

<h3 id="398">[398] Beyond Linear Superposition: Discovering Climate Features in AI Weather Models with KAN-SAE</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17493](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17493) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Minjong Cheon
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Weather Forecasting, Mechanistic Interpretability, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 本文引入KAN-SAE，一种编码器使用可学习B样条激活的稀疏自编码器，用于天气预测模型的机制可解释性。该方法比线性基线多发现72%的特征，并在无气候监督的情况下识别出可解释的欧洲热浪特征和西太平洋台风追踪器。

<h3 id="399">[399] A Distributional View for Visual Mechanistic Interpretability: KL-Minimal Soft-Constraint Principle</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17504](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17504) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guancheng Zhou, Yisi Luo, Zhengfu He, Zhenyu Jin, Xuyang Ge, Wentao Shu, Deyu Meng, Xipeng Qiu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Vision Transformer, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 本文为视觉机制可解释性建立了理论分布视角，将特征激活对自然图像分布的影响建模为KL最小优化问题。提出的方法通过软约束原则平衡可解释性和忠实性，解决了现有范式中的统计偏差问题。

<h3 id="400">[400] BESplit: Bias-Compensated Split Federated Learning with Evidential Aggregation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17508](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17508) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuhan Xie, Chen Lyu, Jingrong Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Knowledge Distillation, Distributed Training
- **Summary**: 本文提出BESplit，一个利用分割联邦学习固有结构来缓解非IID效应的框架。通过证据聚合、偏差补偿协作和双教师蒸馏，该方法在多种非IID设置下在准确性、收敛稳定性和计算效率方面持续优于最先进方法。

<h3 id="401">[401] SaaSBench: Exploring the Boundaries of Coding Agents in Long-Horizon Enterprise SaaS Engineering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17526](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17526) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qingnan Ren, Shun Zou, Shiting Huang, Ziao Zhang, Kou Shi, Zhen Fang, Yiming Zhao, Yu Zeng, Qisheng Su, Lin Chen, Yong Wang, Zehui Chen, Xiangxiang Chu, Feng Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Code Generation, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ShadeCloak/SaaSbench)
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了SaaSBench，首个用于评估AI代理在企业SaaS工程中能力边界的基准测试。该基准涵盖6个SaaS领域的30个复杂任务，包含8种编程语言、6种数据库和13种框架，实验发现代理的主要瓶颈在于系统配置和集成而非代码生成。

<h3 id="402">[402] CasualSynth: Generating Structurally Sound Synthetic Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17528](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17528) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zehua Cheng, Wei Dai, Jiahao Sun, Thomas Lukasiewicz
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Data Synthesis, Causal Inference, LLM
- **Summary**: 本文提出CausalSynth框架，用于生成结构有效的合成数据。该框架将因果结构生成与语义实现解耦，通过结构因果模型生成因果骨架，再由LLM将其映射为高维观测，解决了LLM倾向于用预训练先验覆盖因果事实的语义后门问题。

<h3 id="403">[403] Few-Shot Network Intrusion Detection Using Online Triplet Mining</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17530](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17530) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jack Wilkie, Hanan Hindy, Christos Tachtatzis, Miroslav Bures, Robert Atkinson
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.NI
- **Tags**: Few-Shot Learning, Intrusion Detection, Cybersecurity
- **Venue**: Applied Sciences 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/jackwilkie/few_shot_nids_triplet_mining)
- **Summary**: 本文提出一种基于在线三元组挖掘的三元组网络，用于少样本网络入侵检测。该方法仅需少量恶意样本即可实现有效检测，在每类仅10个恶意样本的情况下达到了与现有方法相当的性能。

<h3 id="404">[404] Rethinking Code Review in the Age of AI: A Vision for Agentic Code Review</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17548](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17548) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hüseyin Özgür Kamalı, Erdem Tuna, Vahid Haratian, Eray Tüzün
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI

- **Venue**: ICSE 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文回顾了代码审查的演变历程，提出了一个结合专业代理与人工质量把关的AI驱动代码审查工作流愿景。该框架涵盖PR创建、增强、审查者选择、AI辅助审查和回顾五个阶段，并讨论了可靠性、偏见、隐私等挑战。

<h3 id="405">[405] Visual Sculpting: Visually-Aligned Planning Representations for Long-Horizon Robot Clay Sculpting</h3>

- **Authors**: Peter Schaldenbrand, Jean Oh
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Deformable Object Manipulation, 3D Vision
- **Venue**: RA-L 2026

<h3 id="406">[406] Controlling False Discovery in Arbitrarily Structured Hypothesis Spaces via Reproducing Kernels</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17559](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17559) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: stat.ME; cs.AI; q-bio.QM; stat.ML

<h3 id="407">[407] Automated Root-Cause Subclassification and No-Code Fix Generation for Invalid Bug Reports</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17561](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17561) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="408">[408] Beyond Accuracy: Robustness, Interpretability and Expressiveness of EEG Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17562](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17562) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Foundation Model, EEG Processing, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 本文从鲁棒性、可解释性和表达能力三个维度对六个EEG基础模型进行了基准测试。研究发现没有单一模型在所有失效模式下都占优，模型在扰动下仍关注正确脑区但解码内容被破坏。

<h3 id="409">[409] UniAlign: A Model-Agnostic Framework for Robust Network Traffic Classification under Distribution Shifts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17575](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17575) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tongze Wang, Xiaohui Xie, Wenduo Wang, Chuyi Wang, Yong Cui
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Traffic Classification, Domain Adaptation, Distribution Shift
- **Summary**: 本文提出UniAlign框架，一种模型无关的方法，用于提升分布偏移下网络流量分类的鲁棒性。该方法结合域对齐微调和稳定模型集成，在减少训练时间的同时提高了分类准确率和F1分数。

<h3 id="410">[410] Bayesian-Monte Carlo Schedule Updating for Construction Digital Twins: A Probabilistic Framework for Dynamic Project Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17608](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17608) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Atena Khoshkonesh, Mohsen Mohammadagha, Vinayak Kaushal, Navid Ebrahimi
- **Subjects**: cs.CE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Digital Twin, Bayesian Optimization, Construction AI
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种用于建筑数字孪生的贝叶斯-蒙特卡洛进度更新框架。该方法整合随机活动持续时间建模、贝叶斯递归更新和蒙特卡洛模拟，实现了自适应的项目进度预测和风险评估。

<h3 id="411">[411] SynVA: A Modular Toolkit for Vessel Generation and Aneurysm Editing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17620](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17620) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Marten J. Finck, Niklas C. Koser, Sarker M. Mahfuz, Tameem Jahangir, Jon E. Wilhelm, Daniel Behme, Naomi Larsen, Wojtek Palubicki, Sylvia Saalfeld, Sören Pirk
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG

- **Summary**: 本文提出SynVA工具包，用于血管网格生成和解剖学一致的动脉瘤合成。该工具结合基于流匹配的健康血管网格生成方法和基于学习的动脉瘤网格生成，发布了50,000个标注网格样本数据集。

<h3 id="412">[412] Multi-task learning on partially labeled datasets via invariant/equivariant semi-supervised learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17624](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17624) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 本文研究了不变性和等变性半监督学习在部分标注数据集上训练多任务模型的潜力。使用FixMatch和Dense FixMatch方法，在目标检测和语义分割任务上超越了监督基线，尤其在标注样本较少时改进显著。

<h3 id="413">[413] SparseSAM: Structured Sparsification of Activations in Segment Anything Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17633](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17633) (cross-listed)

- **Tags**: Model Compression, Vision Transformer, Image Segmentation
- **Summary**: 本文提出SparseSAM，一种无需训练的结构化稀疏化框架，用于加速Segment Anything Model。该方法引入Stripe-Sort Attention和残差一致性MLP，在仅损失0.004 mIoU的情况下实现2倍推理加速和2.8倍内存减少。

<h3 id="414">[414] LLMForge: Multi-Backend Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search with Infinite-Head Attention for Edge Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17653](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17653) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neural Architecture Search, LLM Inference, Edge Computing
- **Summary**: 本文提出LLMForge，一个硬件感知的神经架构搜索框架，用于边缘语言模型。该框架引入无限头注意力机制，结合多后端硬件成本模型，生成针对准确率、能耗和延迟优化的部署感知架构变体。

<h3 id="415">[415] Training Infinitely Deep and Wide Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17660](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17660) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 本文建立了在平均场机制下分析Transformer梯度训练的数学框架，其中深度和宽度趋于无穷。研究证明了前向传播的适定性，并在NTK单射性假设下证明了梯度流收敛到全局最小值。

<h3 id="416">[416] PEIRA: Learning Predictive Encoders through Inter-View Regressor Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17671](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17671) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出PEIRA，一种具有显式目标的非对比自监督学习方法。该方法通过最优线性回归器的迹定义目标，证明其稳定均衡点与典型相关子空间对齐，在ImageNet和CIFAR-10上取得了有竞争力的结果。

<h3 id="417">[417] PULSE: Agentic Investigation with Passive Sensing for Proactive Intervention in Cancer Survivorship</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17679](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17679) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="418">[418] Attention-Guided Fusion of 1D and 2D CNNs for Robust ECG-Based Biometric Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17685](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17685) (cross-listed)

- **Tags**: Biometric Security, ECG Analysis, Multi-Modal Fusion
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种融合1D和2D CNN的混合框架用于ECG生物识别，通过注意力引导的融合机制动态加权两种模态。该方法在三个基准数据集上达到99%以上的识别准确率，并在跨越十年的多会话数据上验证了长期生物特征稳定性。

<h3 id="419">[419] Validate Your Authority: Benchmarking LLMs on Multi-Label Precedent Treatment Classification</h3>

<h3 id="420">[420] Fine-tuning Pocket-Aware Diffusion Models via Denoising Policy Optimization</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了DEPPA方法，利用强化学习微调口袋感知扩散模型进行基于结构的分子优化。该方法可同时优化结合亲和力、药物相似性、可合成性和多样性等多个属性，在CrossDocked2020基准上超越基线方法。

<h3 id="421">[421] Domain Incremental Learning for Pandemic-Resilient Chest X-Ray Analysis</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种基于重放的领域增量持续学习方法，用于跨临床领域的肺炎X光检测。该方法结合类别感知平衡重放和损失加权，在领域偏移数据集上达到88.66%的平均准确率，有效解决了灾难性遗忘问题。

<h3 id="422">[422] L-Drive: Beyond a Single Mapping-Latent Context Drives Time Series Forecasting</h3>

<h3 id="423">[423] Bridging the Version Gap: Multi-version Training Improves ICD Code Prediction, Especially for Rare Codes</h3>

<h3 id="424">[424] OSCAR: Offline Spectral Covariance-Aware Rotation for 2-bit KV Cache Quantization</h3>

<h3 id="425">[425] Systematic Evaluation of the Quality of Synthetic Clinical Notes Rephrased by LLMs at Million-Note Scale</h3>

<h3 id="426">[426] SocialMemBench: Are AI Memory Systems Ready for Social Group Settings?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17789](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17789) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="427">[427] Optimal Knock-Pick Planning for Tightly Packed Tabletop Blocks With Parallel Grippers</h3>

<h3 id="428">[428] Curriculum Group Policy Optimization: Adaptive Sampling for Unleashing the Potential of Text-to-Image Generation</h3>

<h3 id="429">[429] One Model, Two Roles: Emergent Specialization in a Shared Recurrent Transformer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17811](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17811) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="430">[430] Virtues of Ordered Chaos: Planning with Topple Actions in Tabletop Stack Rearrangement</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17815](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17815) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="431">[431] TierCheck: Tiered Checkpointing for Fault Tolerance in Large Language Model Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17821](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17821) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="432">[432] Why We Look Where We Look: Emergent Human-like Fixations of a Foveated Visual Language Model Maximizing Scene Understanding</h3>

<h3 id="433">[433] CounterCount: A Diagnostic Framework for Counting Bias in Vision Language Models</h3>

<h3 id="434">[434] Content-Style Identification via Differential Independence</h3>

<h3 id="435">[435] Efficient Bilevel Optimization for Meta Label Correction in Noisy Label Learning</h3>

<h3 id="436">[436] Temporal Aware Pruning for Efficient Diffusion-based Video Generation</h3>

<h3 id="437">[437] Balancing Knowledge Distillation for Imbalance Learning with Bilevel Optimization</h3>

<h3 id="438">[438] Generating Pretraining Tokens from Organic Data for Data-Bound Scaling</h3>

<h3 id="439">[439] PAREDA: A Multi-Accent Speech Dataset of Natural Language Processing Research Discussions</h3>

<h3 id="440">[440] $\boldsymbol{f}$-OPD: Stabilizing Long-Horizon On-Policy Distillation with Freshness-Aware Control</h3>

<h3 id="441">[441] HINT-SD: Targeted Hindsight Self-Distillation for Long-Horizon Agents</h3>

- **Summary**: 针对长视野LLM代理训练中稀疏奖励的挑战，本文提出了HINT-SD框架，利用全轨迹后见之明选择与失败相关的动作进行针对性的自蒸馏。实验表明，该方法在提高训练效率的同时，显著提升了代理在长视野任务上的表现。

<h3 id="442">[442] Guard: Scalable Straggler Detection and Node Health Management for Large-Scale Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17879](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17879) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guanliang Liu, Abhinandan Patni, Congzhu Lin, Zoe Zeng, Jack Wittmayer, Josh Wu, Ashvin Nihalani, Binxuan Huang, Yinghong Liu, Rory Na, Anthony Ko, Alexander Zhipa, Cong Cheng, Mi Sun, Vijay Rajakumar, Rejith George Joseph, Parthasarathy Govindarajen
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Distributed Training, Hardware Reliability, LLM Training
- **Venue**: MLSys 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了Guard系统，用于大规模训练集群中的落后节点检测和节点健康管理。该系统结合在线性能监控和离线节点扫描机制，将平均FLOPs利用率提升至多1.7倍，训练步方差从20%降至1%。

<h3 id="443">[443] Multi-agent AI systems outperform human teams in creativity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17885](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17885) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tiancheng Hu, Yixuan Jiang, Haotian Li, José Hernández-Orallo, Xing Xie, Nigel Collier, David Stillwell, Luning Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Evaluation, Creativity Assessment
- **Summary**: 该论文研究表明多智能体LLM团队在创造力任务上显著超越人类团队（Cohen's d=1.50），这一优势由新颖性驱动。研究分析了对话动态，发现LLM团队受益于高效探索，而人类团队受益于流畅的对话过程。

<h3 id="444">[444] Attention Sinks and Outliers in Attention Residuals</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17887](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17887) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haozheng Luo, Haoran Dai, Shaoyang Zhang, Xi Chen, Eric Hanchen Jiang, Yijiang Li, Jingyuan Huang, Chenghao Qiu, Chenwei Xu, Zhenyu Pan, Haotian Zhang, Binghui Wang, Yan Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantization, Transformer Architecture, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了OASIS技术，通过层间空信号解决AttnResidual架构中的注意力汇和激活异常值问题。该方法在W8A8量化下困惑度降低75.85%，在W4A4量化下GSM8K Pass@1提升12.42%。

<h3 id="445">[445] DCFold: Efficient Protein Structure Generation with Single Forward Pass</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17899](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17899) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhe Zhang, Yuanning Feng, Yuxuan Song, Keyue Qiu, Hao Zhou, Wei-Ying Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; q-bio.QM
- **Tags**: Protein Engineering, Generative Model, Efficient Architecture
- **Summary**: 本文提出了DCFold，一种单步生成模型，旨在通过单次前向传播实现与AlphaFold3相当的蛋白质结构预测精度。该方法引入了双重一致性训练框架，在保持预测保真度的同时实现了15倍的推理加速。

<h3 id="446">[446] One Model to Translate Them All: Universal Any-to-Any Translation for Heterogeneous Collaborative Perception</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17907](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17907) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yang Li, Weize Li, Quan Yuan, Congzhang Shao, Guiyang Luo, Yunqi Ba, Xuanhan Zhu, Xinyuan Ding, Xiaoyuan Fu, Jinglin Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Sensor Fusion, Domain Adaptation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/CheeryLeeyy/UniTrans)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了UniTrans通用任意到任意特征模态翻译模型，用于解决协同感知中的特征模态异构性问题。该方法预训练翻译器专家参数库，并根据源到目标模态映射学习组合系数，实现零样本翻译器实例化。

<h3 id="447">[447] Domain Transfer Becomes Identifiable via a Single Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17918](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17918) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sagar Shrestha, Subash Timilsina, Hoang-Son Nguyen, Xiao Fu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Domain Adaptation, Representation Learning, Transfer Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文证明了在雅可比支持模式的稀疏性条件下，仅需单个配对锚点样本即可实现域迁移的可识别性。作者提出了基于随机掩码有限差分的高效雅可比稀疏正则化器，在合成和真实域迁移任务上验证了理论。

<h3 id="448">[448] AdaptiveLoad: Towards Efficient Video Diffusion Transformer Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17923](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17923) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yucheng Guo, Yongjian Guo, Zhong Guan, Haoran Sun, Wen Huang, Wanting Xu, Jing Long, Shuai Di, Junwu Xiong
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Diffusion Model, Distributed Training
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了AdaptiveLoad优化框架，用于高效视频扩散Transformer训练。该方法包含双约束自适应负载均衡系统和融合LayerNorm-Modulate CUDA内核，在Wan 2.1世界模型上实现27.2%的吞吐量提升。

<h3 id="449">[449] Prompt Compression in Diffusion Large Language Models: Evaluating LLMLingua-2 on LLaDA</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17932](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17932) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sterling Huang, Abigayle Brown, Jiyoo Noh, Jiakang Xu, Wantong Huo, Kaung Myat Kyaw, Jonathan Chan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Diffusion Model, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 本文研究了针对自回归LLM设计的提示压缩方法是否能有效迁移到扩散大语言模型(DLLMs)，使用LLMLingua-2在LLaDA模型上进行评估。实验结果表明语义保留并不一定意味着扩散模型中稳定的下游行为，数学推理任务在压缩下显著退化，而摘要任务相对稳健。

<h3 id="450">[450] BacktestBench: Benchmarking Large Language Models for Automated Quantitative Strategy Backtesting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17937](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17937) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhensheng Wang, Wenmian Yang, Qingtai Wu, Lequan Ma, Yiquan Zhang, Weijia Jia
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantitative Finance, Benchmark, LLM Agent
- **Venue**: KDD 2026
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了BacktestBench，首个大规模自动化量化回测基准，包含超过600万条真实市场记录和18,246个标注问答对。作者还提出了AutoBacktest多智能体基线方法，将自然语言策略转化为可复现的回测，并在23个主流LLM上进行了评估。

<h3 id="451">[451] Training data attribution in diffusion models via mirrored unlearning and noise-consistent skew</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17938](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17938) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Joan Serrà, Dipam Goswami, Fabio Morreale, Wei-Hsiang Liao, Yuki Mitsufuji
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Interpretability, Data Attribution
- **Summary**: 本文提出了MUCS方法，通过镜像去学习和噪声一致性偏斜来实现扩散模型的训练数据归因(TDA)。该方法在有界镜像梯度上升的基础上微调第二个模型，并在三个数据集上显著优于现有方法，为生成模型的可解释性提供了新途径。

<h3 id="452">[452] A More Word-like Image Tokenization for MLLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17954](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17954) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hyun Lee, Hyemin Jeong, Yejin Kim, Hyungwook Choi, Hyunsoo Cho, Soo Kyung Kim, Joonseok Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Image Tokenization, Model Compression
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/snuviplab/DiVT)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了DiVT(解耦视觉标记化)方法，将图像块嵌入聚类为连贯的语义单元，使每个标记对应一个独特的视觉概念而非固定的网格单元。该方法能够根据图像复杂度自适应调整标记预算，在显著减少视觉标记数量的同时保持或超越基线性能。

<h3 id="453">[453] BLAgent: Agentic RAG for File-Level Bug Localization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17965](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17965) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Md Afif Al Mamun, Gias Uddin
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Agent, Software Testing
- **Summary**: 本文提出了BLAgent，一个用于文件级错误定位的智能体RAG框架，集成了代码结构感知的仓库编码、双视角查询转换和两阶段智能体重排序。该方法在SWE-bench Lite上实现了超过78%的Top-1准确率，同时成本比最强基线低18倍以上。

<h3 id="454">[454] Babel: Jailbreaking Safety Attention via Obfuscation Distribution Optimized Sampling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17971](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17971) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ziwei Wang, Jing Chen, Ruichao Liang, Zhi Wang, Yebo Feng, Ju Jia, Ruiying Du, Cong Wu, Yang Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Jailbreak Attack, Red Teaming
- **Summary**: 本文研究了LLM安全机制的内在漏洞，发现安全对齐依赖于稀疏分布的注意力头，留下了大部分表示空间监控薄弱。基于此发现，作者提出了Babel黑盒攻击框架，通过系统化的混淆采样实现高效越狱攻击，在GPT-4o和Claude-3-5-haiku上达到了最先进的攻击成功率。

<h3 id="455">[455] SAFE-SVD: Sensitivity-Aware Fidelity-Enforcing SVD for Physics Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17985](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17985) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chengjie Hong, Feixiang He, Yiheng Zeng, Lulu Kang, He Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Scientific Computing, Foundation Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种用于物理基础模型压缩的敏感性感知保真度强制框架，在压缩过程中显式建模输出函数空间中的损失感知层敏感性。该方法在多个模型和数据集上实现了显著更高的压缩比，同时保持准确性和物理保真度，为科学基础模型的高效部署提供了新途径。

<h3 id="456">[456] LivePI: More Realistic Benchmarking of Agents Against Indirect Prompt Injectio</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17986](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17986) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lei Zhao, Abhay Bhaskar, Edgar Dobriban
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Prompt Injection, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了LivePI，一个用于生产环境中间接提示注入(IPI)风险的结构化基准，覆盖七种输入面和十二种攻击族。对多个LLM的评估显示攻击成功率为10.7%-29.6%，群聊注入在所有评估骨干中均成功，同时提出的双层防御能有效拦截恶意目标完成。

<h3 id="457">[457] Predictive Prefetching for Retrieval-Augmented Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17989](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17989) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wuyang Zhang, Shichao Pei
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Inference, Prefetching
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种先进的异步检索框架，通过检索预测器、上下文监控器和查询生成器实现与信息需求对齐的预测性预取。实验表明该方法可实现高达43.5%的端到端延迟降低和62.4%的首令牌时间改善，同时保持与同步RAG基线相当的答案质量。

<h3 id="458">[458] Stable Audio 3</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17991](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17991) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zach Evans, Julian D. Parker, Matthew Rice, CJ Carr, Zack Zukowski, Josiah Taylor, Jordi Pons
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Audio Generation, Diffusion Model, Generative Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Stability-AI/stable-audio-tools), [code](https://github.com/Stability-AI/stable-audio-3)
- **Summary**: 本文发布了Stable Audio 3，一个用于可变长度音频生成和编辑的快速潜在扩散模型系列，支持修复功能以实现目标音频编辑和录音延续。模型采用新颖的语义-声学自编码器和对抗性后训练，可在消费级硬件上高效生成音乐和声音。

<h3 id="459">[459] Towards Sustainable Growth: A Multi-Value-Aware Retrieval Framework for E-Commerce Search</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17994](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17994) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yifan Wang, Yixuan Wang, YiDan Liang, Qiang Liu, Fei Xiao
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Information Retrieval, Generative Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出了GrowthGR，一个面向电商搜索的多价值感知检索框架，通过ItemLTV预测模块和多价值感知生成检索模块来平衡即时转化和长期商品增长。该方法在淘宝生产平台成功部署，实现了新商品GMV 5.3%的提升和整体搜索GMV 0.3%的增长。

<h3 id="460">[460] MARR: Module-Adaptive Residual Reconstruction for Low-Bit Post-Training Quantization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17997](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17997) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Le Su, Xing Luo, Zhi Jin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Quantization, Model Compression, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 本文提出了MARR(模块自适应残差重建)方法用于低位后训练量化，为每个模块分配特定的缩放系数以自适应平衡累积误差校正和残差相关偏差。实验表明该方法在LLM上实现高达20.2%的性能提升，在ViT上实现4.6%的相对提升。

<h3 id="461">[461] Verify-Gated Completion as Admission Control in a Governed Multi-Agent Runtime: A Bounded Architecture Case Study</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17998](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17998) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hai-Duong Nguyen, The-Xuan Tran
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 本文研究了验证门控完成作为治理多智能体运行时的准入控制模式，其中只读验证器决定是否接受完成声明。参考实现在已知结果的调用事件中实现了99.5%的验证成功份额，模糊或证据不足的情况采用失败关闭策略解决。

<h3 id="462">[462] Spiker-LL: An Energy-Efficient FPGA Accelerator Enabling Adaptive Local Learning in Spiking Neural Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18003](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18003) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alessio Caviglia, Filippo Marostica, Alessandro Savino, Stefano Di Carlo
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Spiking Neural Network, FPGA, Edge Computing
- **Summary**: 本文提出了SPIKER-LL，一个基于FPGA的SNN加速器，扩展了开源Spiker+推理架构以支持STSF本地学习规则。该加速器在MNIST、F-MNIST和DIGITS上实现了高达93%的准确率、亚毫秒延迟和每次推理低于0.1 mJ的能耗，适用于边缘FPGA部署。

<h3 id="463">[463] SAS: Semantic-aware Sampling for Generative Dataset Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18012](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18012) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mingzhuo Li, Guang Li, Linfeng Ye, Jiafeng Mao, Takahiro Ogawa, Konstantinos N. Plataniotis, Miki Haseyama
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Dataset Distillation, Data Synthesis, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Venue**: IEEE OJSP
- **Summary**: 本文引入了语义感知视角的数据集蒸馏方法，利用CLIP作为语义先验进行后采样，设计了三个语义评分函数来量化类别相关性、类间可分性和集内多样性。两阶段采样策略在多个数据集和下游模型上展示了持续的性能提升。

<h3 id="464">[464] TinySAM 2: Extreme Memory Compression for Efficient Track Anything Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18013](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18013) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhaoyuan Ding, Yijing Yang, Han Shu, Xinghao Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Segmentation, Model Compression, Image Segmentation
- **Summary**: 本文提出了TinySAM 2，一个轻量级视频分割模型，引入了内存质量管理机制和联合时空标记压缩。该方法仅用7%的内存标记和3%的训练数据即可达到SAM 2.1性能的90%，有效缓解了参数量、计算负载和部署成本的瓶颈。

<h3 id="465">[465] See What I Mean: Aligning Vision and Language Representations for Video Fine-grained Object Understanding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18018](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18018) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Boyuan Sun, Bowen Yin, Yuanming Li, Xihan Wei, Qibin Hou
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Video Understanding, Multimodal Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/HumanMLLM/SWIM)
- **Summary**: SWIM是一种新颖的训练策略，通过在训练时利用掩码监督引导跨模态注意力，实现仅从文本提示进行细粒度物体理解，解决了预训练多模态大语言模型中属性词和物体名词之间的系统性错位问题。

<h3 id="466">[466] Unveiling Memorization-Generalization Coexistence: A Case Study on Arithmetic Tasks with Label Noise</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18022](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18022) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Linyu Liu, Pinyan Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Deep Learning Theory, Generalization Bound
- **Summary**: 本文研究了过参数化模型如何在存在标签噪声的情况下同时实现记忆和泛化，发现更大的模型在适当配置下泛化能力更强，且即使存在80%的标签噪声，也能通过频率方法提取内部泛化结构实现接近完美的测试准确率。

<h3 id="467">[467] Interaction-Breaking Adversarial Learning Framework for Robust Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18024](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18024) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.MA

- **Summary**: 本文提出了交互破坏对抗学习(IBAL)框架，从信息论角度构建攻击来阻碍多智能体协调，并训练智能体在干扰下可靠执行，在多种攻击设置和智能体缺失场景下显著提升了鲁棒性。

<h3 id="468">[468] FedSDR: Federated Self-Distillation with Rectification</h3>

<h3 id="469">[469] Quantum Sidecar Architectures for Hybrid AI Training and Inference: Stateful Protected Registers, Stateless Reset-and-Reprepare Circuits and Quantum Weight-State Outlook</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18031](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18031) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Y.Mo, G.D.Su

- **Tags**: Quantum Computing, Hybrid AI Architecture
- **Summary**: 本文提出了量子边车架构系列，用于未来混合AI训练和推理，包括有状态保护寄存器模式和无状态重置-重准备模式，将量子协处理器定位为有界信号生成器。

<h3 id="470">[470] PROTEA: Offline Evaluation and Iterative Refinement for Multi-Agent LLM Workflows</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18032](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18032) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kazuki Kawamura, Satoshi Waki, Kei Tateno
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.HC; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Agent Debugging
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Summary**: PROTEA是一个用于多智能体LLM工作流离线测试驱动改进的统一界面，通过评分中间节点输出、定位瓶颈和提供针对性提示修订，帮助开发者调试和优化复杂工作流。

<h3 id="471">[471] Exploring Trust Calibration in XAI - The Impact of Exposing Model Limitations to Lay Users</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18036](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18036) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 本文提出ISEP方法用于离线强化学习，通过在行为策略样本和策略样本之间插值的价值函数隐式扩展可行动作支持，使用随机动作选择策略避免模式崩溃。

<h3 id="504">[504] Improved Baselines with Representation Autoencoders</h3>

<h3 id="505">[505] Same Signal, Different Semantics: A Cross-Framework Behavioral Analysis of Software Engineering Agents</h3>

<h3 id="507">[507] Optimising CSRNet with parameter-free attention mechanisms for crowd counting in public transport</h3>

- **Subjects**: stat.ME; cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.AP
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Education Technology
- **Summary**: 该研究评估了大型语言模型作为试题难度评估者的能力，发现在特定配置下（如成对比较和少样本学习），LLM估计的难度与经验难度具有中等到强的正相关性，可作为初始项目校准的有力工具。

<h3 id="538">[538] LongMINT: Evaluating Memory under Multi-Target Interference in Long-Horizon Agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18565](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18565) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hyunji Lee, Justin Chih-Yao Chen, Joykirat Singh, Zaid Khan, Elias Stengel-Eskin, Mohit Bansal
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Benchmark, Long Context
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/amy-hyunji/LongMINT)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了LongMINT基准，用于评估智能体在长视野、高干扰环境下的记忆能力，发现现有系统在处理需要聚合推理的多目标干扰任务时表现不佳，主要受限于检索和记忆构建。

<h3 id="539">[539] Overeager Coding Agents: Measuring Out-of-Scope Actions on Benign Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18583](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18583) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yubin Qu, Ying Zhang, Yanjun Zhang, Gelei Deng, Yuekang Li, Leo Yu Zhang, Yi Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Safety, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了OverEager-Gen基准，用于测量编程智能体在处理良性任务时的过度执行行为，发现移除同意声明会显著增加智能体执行超出范围操作的概率，并分析了不同框架和模型的影响。

<h3 id="540">[540] Randomized Advantage Transformation (RAT): Computing Natural Policy Gradients via Direct Backpropagation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18591](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18591) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mingfei Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Optimization
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了随机优势变换（RAT）方法，通过直接反向传播估计自然策略梯度，避免了昂贵的Fisher矩阵计算，在连续和视觉控制任务中取得了优异的性能。

<h3 id="541">[541] AMARIS: A Memory-Augmented Rubric Improvement System for Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18592](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18592) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Peilin Wu, Xinlu Zhang, Kun Wan, Wentian Zhao, Gang Wu, Xinya Du, Zhiyu Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Alignment, Reward Design
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了AMARIS系统，通过引入持久性评估记忆来改进基于评分标准的强化学习，利用长期训练历史来优化评分标准，从而提供更丰富的奖励信号并提升模型性能。

<h3 id="542">[542] Not What You Asked For: Typographic Attacks in Household Robot Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18593](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18593) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ali Iranmanesh, Peng Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Robotics, Adversarial Robustness, Embodied AI
- **Summary**: 该研究评估了排版攻击对家庭机器人操作的影响，发现攻击者可以通过在场景中放置打印文本来误导机器人的感知，导致其抓取错误物体，揭示了此类攻击对物理安全的具体威胁。

<h3 id="543">[543] CATA: Continual Machine Unlearning via Conflict-Averse Task Arithmetic</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18610](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18610) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shen Lin, Junhao Dong, Rongjie Chen, Xiaoyu Zhang, Li Xu, Xiaofeng Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning, Vision-Language Model, Continual Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了CATA方法，通过冲突避免的任务算术来解决视觉语言模型中的持续机器遗忘问题，有效平衡了遗忘目标知识、保留模型效用和防止知识重现这三个关键挑战。

<h3 id="544">[544] SAME: A Semantically-Aligned Music Autoencoder</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18613](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18613) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Julian D. Parker, Zach Evans, CJ Carr, Zachary Zukowski, Josiah Taylor, Matthew Rice, Jordi Pons
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Music Generation, Audio Processing, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了SAME，一种语义对齐的音乐自编码器，通过结合Transformer骨干网络和语义正则化方法，在保持重建质量的同时实现了4096倍的时域压缩，并降低了计算成本。

<h3 id="545">[545] ManiSoft: Towards Vision-Language Manipulation for Soft Continuum Robotics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18617](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18617) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ziyu Wei, Luting Wang, Chen Gao, Li Wen, Si Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Robotics, Vision-Language Model, Benchmark
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了ManiSoft，一个针对软体机械臂的视觉-语言操作基准，包含模拟器、四个任务和6300个多样化场景及专家轨迹。实验表明策略在干净场景表现良好，但在随机化条件下性能显著下降，主要原因是视觉状态估计不准确和对变形能力的利用有限。

<h3 id="546">[546] Stochastic Penalty-Barrier Methods for Constrained Machine Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18618](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18618) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Adam Bosák, Andrii Kliachkin, Jana Lepšová, Gilles Bareilles, Jakub Mareček
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Optimization, Constrained Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了随机惩罚-障碍方法(SPBM)，将经典惩罚和障碍方法扩展到深度学习中常见的非凸、非光滑、随机优化场景。该方法通过指数对偶平均、稳定化惩罚调度和Moreau包络处理非光滑性，在多个实验中匹配或超越现有约束优化基线。

<h3 id="547">[547] CrossView Suite: Harnessing Cross-view Spatial Intelligence of MLLMs with Dataset, Model and Benchmark</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18621](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18621) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wei Wang, Yuqian Yuan, Tianwei Lin, Wenqiao Zhang, Siliang Tang, Jun Xiao, Yueting Zhuang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Multimodal Learning, Spatial Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Thinkirin/Crossview-Suite)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了CrossView Suite，包含大规模跨视图指令数据集CrossViewSet（160万样本）、综合基准CrossViewBench和渐进式框架CrossViewer，用于提升多模态大语言模型的跨视图空间推理能力。该方法通过自适应空间区域标记器和显式跨视图对齐机制，显著提升了模型的空间智能。

<h3 id="548">[548] Position: Weight Space Should Be a First-Class Generative AI Modality</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18632](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18632) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhangyang Wang, Peihao Wang, Kai Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Foundation Model, Generative Model, Weight Space Generation
- **Summary**: 本文主张将神经网络检查点视为一等数据模态，将权重空间生成建模标准化为核心机器学习原语。作者将现有方法组织为五阶段流水线，指出适配器规模和条件生成进展迅速，而无限制的前沿规模检查点合成仍是开放问题。

<h3 id="549">[549] Data Presentation Over Architecture: Resampling Strategies for Credit Risk Prediction with Tabular Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18635](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18635) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aditya Tanna, Mitul Solanki, Mohamed Bouadi, Nassim Bouarour, Pratinav Seth, Vinay Kumar Sankarapu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Tabular Learning, Foundation Model, Class Imbalance
- **Summary**: 本文在信用风险预测任务上对比了表格基础模型(TFM)和经典模型，发现上下文构建策略比模型架构选择更能解释AUC-ROC的方差。平衡和混合采样策略比均匀采样提升3-4个AUC点，使TFM在仅使用5K-10K样本时即可达到经典基线的性能。

<h3 id="550">[550] Post-Trained MoE Can Skip Half Experts via Self-Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18643](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18643) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xingtai Lv, Li Sheng, Kaiyan Zhang, Yichen You, Siyan Gao, Xueheng Luo, Yuxin Zuo, Yuchen Fan, Junlin Yang, Ganqu Cui, Bingning Wang, Fan Yang, Youbang Sun, Ning Ding, Bowen Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, Knowledge Distillation, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 本文提出了ZEDA框架，通过注入零输出专家和两阶段自蒸馏，将训练后的静态MoE模型转换为高效的动态模型。在Qwen3-30B-A3B和GLM-4.7-Flash上，该方法消除了超过50%的专家FLOPs，同时保持边际精度损失，实现了约1.20倍的推理加速。

<h3 id="551">[551] An Assessment of Human vs. Model Uncertainty in Soft-Label Learning and Calibration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18648](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18648) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Maja Pavlovic, Silviu Paun, Massimo Poesio
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Uncertainty Estimation, Model Calibration, Data Annotation
- **Summary**: 本文在MNIST和合成数据集上对软标签学习进行了受控审计，将软标签监督与标签模式偏移解耦。研究发现人类软标签的主要价值在于作为正则化器改善困难样本的模型校准并促进训练收敛稳定性，使模型能够反映人类不确定性。

<h3 id="552">[552] Pocket Foundation Models: Distilling TFMs into CPU-Ready Gradient-Boosted Trees</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18654](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18654) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aditya Tanna, Nassim Bouarour, Mohamed Bouadi, Vinay kumar Sankarapu, Pratinav Seth
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Tabular Learning, Foundation Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出将表格基础模型(TFM)蒸馏为可在CPU上运行的XGBoost或CatBoost学生模型，使用分层袋外教师标注防止标签泄漏。在153个分类数据集上，蒸馏后的模型达到教师96.5%的AUC，在CPU上运行仅需1.9毫秒，实现38倍至860倍的加速。

<h3 id="553">[553] Statistical Limits and Efficient Algorithms for Differentially Private Federated Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18656](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18656) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arnab Auddy, Xiangni Peng, Subhadeep Paul
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.ME
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Differential Privacy, Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文研究了差分隐私联邦学习M估计中估计精度、隐私约束和通信成本之间的权衡，提出了FedHybrid和FedNewton方法。FedNewton通过平均局部牛顿迭代减少FedAvg的偏差，在客户端数量增长较慢时以更少的通信轮次达到与FedSGD相当的估计精度。

<h3 id="554">[554] KairosHope: A Next-Generation Time-Series Foundation Model for Specialized Classification via Dual-Memory Architecture</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18657](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18657) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Luis Balderas, José Alberto Rodríguez, Miguel Lastra, Antonio Arauzo-Azofra, José M. Benítez
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Classification, Foundation Model, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了KairosHope，一种面向时间序列分类任务的下一代基础模型，其核心是HOPE块架构，采用双记忆系统（Titans模块用于短期记忆，Continuum Memory System用于长期历史上下文抽象）替代二次注意力机制。该模型在Monash数据集上进行自监督预训练，结合掩码时间序列建模和对比学习，并通过线性探测和全量微调协议在UCR基准数据集上取得了优异性能。

<h3 id="555">[555] COOPO: Cyclic Offline-Online Policy Optimization Algorithm</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18675](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18675) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qisai Liu, Zhanhong Jiang, Joshua Russell Waite, Aditya Balu, Cody Fleming, Soumik Sarkar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Classification, Foundation Model, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了KairosHope，一个用于时间序列分类的基础模型，采用HOPE块替代二次注意力，包含双记忆系统（Titans模块用于短期记忆，Continuum Memory System用于长期上下文）。模型在Monash数据集上进行自监督预训练，结合混合决策头融合深度表示和统计特征，在UCR基准上表现优异。

<h3 id="556">[556] Lance: Unified Multimodal Modeling by Multi-Task Synergy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18678](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18678) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fengyi Fu, Mengqi Huang, Shaojin Wu, Yunsheng Jiang, Yufei Huo, Hao Li, Yinghang Song, Fei Ding, Jianzhu Guo, Qian He, Zheren Fu, Zhendong Mao, Yongdong Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Mixture-of-Experts, Video Generation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/bytedance/Lance)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Lance，一个轻量级统一多模态模型，支持图像和视频的理解、生成和编辑。该模型采用双流混合专家架构，通过统一上下文建模和解耦能力路径实现多任务协同训练，在图像和视频生成任务上显著优于现有开源统一模型。

<h3 id="557">[557] Reversa: A Reverse Documentation Engineering Framework for Converting Legacy Software into Operational Specifications for AI Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18684](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18684) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sanderson Oliveira de Macedo, Ronaldo Martins da Costa
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Software Documentation, Program Analysis
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Reversa框架，通过多智能体流水线将遗留软件转换为可追溯的操作规范供AI智能体使用。框架强调代码与规范之间的可追溯性、显式置信度标记和保留空白供人工验证，并通过COBOL到Go的ATM迁移案例研究进行了验证。

<h3 id="558">[558] Ensembling Tabular Foundation Models - A Diversity Ceiling And A Calibration Trap</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18696](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18696) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aditya Tanna, Yash Desai, Pratinav Seth, Mohamed Bouadi, Nassim Bouarour, Vinay Kumar Sankarapu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Tabular Learning, Foundation Model, Ensemble Learning, Model Calibration
- **Summary**: 本文在153个OpenML分类任务上对六种表格基础模型的集成策略进行了基准测试，发现TFM形成近乎冗余的池（平均成对Q统计量为0.961）。最佳集成策略仅带来+0.18%的精度提升但需要253倍的计算开销，且堆叠方法在提升精度的同时破坏了校准性。

<h3 id="559">[559] PopPy: Opportunistically Exploiting Parallelism in Python Compound AI Applications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18697](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18697) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Stephen Mell, David Mell, Konstantinos Kallas, Steve Zdancewic, Osbert Bastani
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.PL
- **Tags**: LLM Application, Parallel Computing, Compiler Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文开发了PopPy系统，用于发现Python复合AI应用中调用外部组件时的并行化机会。该系统结合提前编译器和运行时，解决语言复杂性、动态分发和变量变异等挑战，在真实复合AI应用上实现了高达6.4倍的端到端加速。

<h3 id="560">[560] Distilling Tabular Foundation Models for Structured Health Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18702](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18702) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aditya Tanna, Nassim Bouarour, Mohamed Bouadi, Vinay Kumar Sankarapu, Pratinav Seth
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Tabular Learning, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 本文研究了将表格基础模型蒸馏为轻量级表格模型用于医疗数据，使用分层袋外教师标注防止上下文泄漏。在19个医疗数据集上，蒸馏后的学生模型保留了至少90%的教师AUC，在CPU上运行速度至少快26倍，同时保持了医疗应用关键的校准性和公平性。

<h3 id="561">[561] Semantic Generative Tuning for Unified Multimodal Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18714](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18714) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Songsong Yu, Yuxin Chen, Ying Shan, Yanwei Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Multimodal Learning, Image Segmentation
- **Summary**: 本文研究了统一多模态模型(UMMs)的生成式后训练，发现图像分割等高级语义任务可作为最优代理来桥接视觉理解与生成之间的隔离。作者提出语义生成调优(SGT)范式，利用分割作为生成代理来对齐和协同多模态能力，显著提升了多模态理解和生成保真度。

<h3 id="562">[562] DexHoldem: Playing Texas Hold'em with Dexterous Embodied System</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18727](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18727) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Feng Chen, Tianzhe Chu, Li Sun, Pei Zhou, Zhuxiu Xu, Shenghua Gao, Yuexiang Zhai, Yanchao Yang, Yi Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Robotics, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了DexHoldem，一个基于德州扑克灵巧操作的真实世界系统级基准，包含1470个遥操作演示、标准化物理策略基准和感知基准。实验发现最佳模型在原始执行上获得61.2%的任务完成率，但在感知和策略错误的累积下仍存在显著差距。

<h3 id="563">[563] Predictable Confabulations: Factual Recall by LLMs Scales with Model Size and Topic Frequency</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18732](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18732) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Matthew L. Smith, Jonathan P. Shock, Samuel T. Segun, Iyiola E. Olatunji, Tegawendé F. Bissyandé
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, LLM Hallucination, Knowledge Retrieval
- **Summary**: 本研究评估了38个模型在超过8900个学术引用上的表现，发现事实回忆质量遵循模型参数数量和训练数据中主题表示的对数线性组合的sigmoid函数。这两个变量解释了60%的方差，支持了一种信号噪声比的观点：信号强度随概念频率增长，噪声底限随模型容量增长。

<h3 id="564">[564] Vision-OPD: Learning to See Fine Details for Multimodal LLMs via On-Policy Self-Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18740](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18740) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qianhao Yuan, Jie Lou, Xing Yu, Hongyu Lin, Le Sun, Xianpei Han, Yaojie Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Knowledge Distillation, Fine-Grained Recognition
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/VisionOPD/Vision-OPD)
- **Summary**: 本文提出Vision-OPD，一种区域到全局的自蒸馏框架，用于提升多模态大语言模型的细粒度视觉理解能力。该方法通过将模型自身的区域感知能力转移到全图像策略，无需外部教师模型、真实标签或推理时工具使用即可实现性能提升。

<h3 id="565">[565] ESI-Bench: Towards Embodied Spatial Intelligence that Closes the Perception-Action Loop</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18746](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18746) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yining Hong, Jiageng Liu, Han Yin, Manling Li, Leonidas Guibas, Li Fei-Fei, Jiajun Wu, Yejin Choi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Benchmark, Spatial Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文引入ESI-BENCH，一个全面的具身空间智能基准，涵盖10个任务类别和29个子类别，代理必须主动决定如何部署感知、运动和操作能力。实验发现大多数失败源于动作盲目而非感知薄弱，模型过早做出高置信度承诺，暴露了元认知差距。

<h3 id="566">[566] Code as Agent Harness</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18747](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18747) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="567">[567] DashAttention: Differentiable and Adaptive Sparse Hierarchical Attention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18753](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18753) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Sparse Attention, LLM Inference, Long Context
- **Summary**: 本文提出DashAttention，一种可微分自适应稀疏分层注意力机制，利用α-entmax变换根据当前查询选择可变数量的KV块。该方法在75%稀疏度下达到与全注意力相当的准确率，并在Triton中实现了高效的GPU实现，推理时比FlashAttention-3更快。

<h3 id="569">[569] A Machine with Short-Term, Episodic, and Semantic Memory Systems</h3>

<h3 id="570">[570] Principles of frugal inference and control</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文引入了一种新的POMDP框架变体，将推理获取的信息视为需要与效用一起优化的资源。通过求解局部线性高斯近似问题，揭示了资源高效控制的三个原则：推理从无损压缩转向有损策略、存在等价解流形、控制可用于抵消估计误差。

<h3 id="571">[571] Designing Cellular Manufacturing System in Presence of Alternative Process Plans</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出了四种整数规划公式，用于在存在替代工艺计划的单元制造系统中进行零件和机器分组。公式通过将零件类型的最大可能连续操作分配到同一单元和同一机器，实现单元间和单元内移动的最小化。

<h3 id="572">[572] Automated Knowledge Component Generation for Interpretable Knowledge Tracing in Coding Problems</h3>

<h3 id="573">[573] Action-Gradient Monte Carlo Tree Search for Non-Parametric Continuous (PO)MDPs</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.RO

<h3 id="574">[574] A Survey on Foundation Models for Personalized Federated Intelligence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.06907](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.06907) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yu Qiao, Huy Q. Le, Avi Deb Raha, Phuong-Nam Tran, Apurba Adhikary, Mengchun Zhang, Loc X. Nguyen, Eui-Nam Huh, Dusit Niyato, Choong Seon Hong

<h3 id="575">[575] Evaluating AI Alignment in LLMs: Output Analysis of Value Priorities Across 75 Models with Human Benchmarking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.12617](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.12617) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Gabriel Rongyang Lau, Wei Yan Low, Seow Min Koh, Fiona Fui-Hoon Nah, Andree Hartanto
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, LLM Evaluation, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: 本文引入了一种基于输出的AI对齐评估方法，将LLM生成的文本视为行为数据，比较表达的价值优先级与人类参考。研究对75个LLM和376名人类受访者进行了基准测试，发现模型可能看似对齐但仍与人类价值校准存在偏差，且代理能力被系统性地低估。

<h3 id="576">[576] OSWorld-Human: Benchmarking the Efficiency of Computer-Use Agents</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.OS

<h3 id="577">[577] CooT: Learning to Coordinate In-Context with Coordination Transformers</h3>

<h3 id="588">[588] WELD: The First Naturalistic Long-Period Small-Team Workplace Emotion Dataset for Ubiquitous Affective Computing</h3>

<h3 id="589">[589] Scales++: Compute Efficient Evaluation Subset Selection with Cognitive Scales Embeddings</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文介绍了AgroCoT，一个专为农业场景设计的视觉问答数据集，集成了思维链推理来评估视觉语言模型的推理能力。通过对30个代表性VLM的评估，揭示了模型在零样本场景下推理能力的差距，强调了CoT评估的重要性。

<h3 id="594">[594] What Drives Success in Physical Planning with Joint-Embedding Predictive World Models?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.24497](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24497) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Basile Terver, Tsung-Yen Yang, Jean Ponce, Adrien Bardes, Yann LeCun
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.RO; stat.ML
- **Tags**: World Model, Model-Based RL, Robotics
- **Venue**: TMLR
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/facebookresearch/jepa-wms)
- **Summary**: 本文研究了JEPA-WMs这类在世界模型学习表示空间中进行规划的方法，通过模拟环境和真实机器人数据实验，分析了模型架构、训练目标和规划算法对规划成功的影响，提出了优于DINO-WM和V-JEPA-2-AC基线的模型。

<h3 id="595">[595] FormuLLA: A Large Language Model Approach to Generating Novel 3D Printable Formulations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.02071](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.02071) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Adeshola Okubena, Yusuf Ali Mohammed, Moe Elbadawi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Drug Discovery, Fine-Tuning, Material Discovery
- **Summary**: 本文研究了将大语言模型微调用于药物3D打印配方推荐的方法，在包含1400多个配方的FDM数据集上进行实验。研究发现较小LLM会出现灾难性遗忘，且标准LLM指标无法有效评估配方可加工性。

<h3 id="596">[596] The Homogenization Problem in LLMs: Towards Meaningful Diversity in AI Safety</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.06116](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06116) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ian Rios-Sialer
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Bias Mitigation, Fairness
- **Summary**: 本文论证了同质化（多样性丧失）应成为AI安全的核心关注点，提出了一个框架来刻画LLM中的同质化问题，并引入xeno-reproduction概念来促进多样性。作者通过实验展示了Claude 3.5 Haiku中的性别偏见。

<h3 id="597">[597] MirrorBench: A Benchmark to Evaluate Conversational User-Proxy Agents for Human-Likeness</h3>

<h3 id="598">[598] PersonaDual: Balancing Personalization and Objectivity via Adaptive Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.08679](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.08679) (replaced)

- **Summary**: 本文提出了PersonaDual框架，支持单一模型同时进行通用客观推理和个性化推理，并根据上下文自适应切换模式。该框架通过SFT和DualGRPO强化学习优化模式选择，在保持个性化优势的同时减少干扰。

<h3 id="599">[599] Agentic AI Governance and Lifecycle Management in Healthcare</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.15630](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.15630) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chandra Prakash, Mary Lind, Avneesh Sisodia
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Medical AI, AI Governance
- **Summary**: 本文提出了统一代理生命周期管理(UALM)蓝图，用于医疗组织中代理AI系统的治理。该框架将治理差距映射到五个控制层，包括身份注册、编排协调、PHI边界上下文、运行时策略执行和生命周期管理。

<h3 id="600">[600] Inference-Time Diversity in RL-Trained Lean Theorem Provers: A Diagnostic Study</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.16172](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16172) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Automated Theorem Proving, Reinforcement Learning, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文揭示了RL训练的Lean定理证明器在推理时存在模式崩溃问题，发现使用策略骨架可以打破这一瓶颈并获得45%的相对提升。研究表明这一现象是RL特有的，推理时结构多样性是RL训练证明器的有效补充方法。

<h3 id="601">[601] When Personalization Legitimizes Risks: Uncovering Safety Vulnerabilities in Personalized Dialogue Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.17887](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.17887) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiahe Guo, Xiangran Guo, Yulin Hu, Zimo Long, Xingyu Sui, Xuda Zhi, Yongbo Huang, Hao He, Weixiang Zhao, Yanyan Zhao, Bing Qin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, LLM Personalization, LLM Agent
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/MuyuenLP/PS-Bench)
- **Summary**: 本文揭示了个性化代理中的意图合法化安全漏洞，即良性个人记忆会导致模型将有害查询合法化。作者引入PS-Bench基准进行量化评估，发现个性化使攻击成功率增加15.8%-243.7%，并提出了检测-反思方法来降低安全风险。

<h3 id="602">[602] MemOCR: Layout-Aware Visual Memory for Efficient Long-Horizon Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.21468](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21468) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI

<h3 id="603">[603] Enhancing Table Reasoning with Deterministic Table-State Rewards</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.22530](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22530) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tung Sum Thomas Kwok, Xinyu Wang, Hengzhi He, Xiaofeng Lin, Peng Lu, Liheng Ma, Chunhe Wang, Chun Ho Mak, Yuyu Luo, Ying Nian Wu, Lei Ding, Guang Cheng

<h3 id="604">[604] Real-Time Aligned Reward Model beyond Semantics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.22664](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22664) (replaced)

- **Summary**: 本文提出了R2M框架，一种轻量级RLHF方法，利用策略模型的实时反馈来对齐策略分布变化。该方法超越了仅依赖语义表示的传统奖励模型，通过实时利用策略模型反馈来缓解奖励过度优化问题。

<h3 id="605">[605] Supervised sparse auto-encoders for interpretable and compositional representations</h3>

<h3 id="606">[606] Hunt Instead of Wait: Evaluating Deep Data Research on Large Language Models</h3>

<h3 id="607">[607] Mitigating Conversational Inertia in Multi-Turn Agents</h3>

- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文识别了多轮代理中的对话惯性现象，即模型错误地将自身先前响应作为少样本示例模仿。作者提出了上下文偏好学习方法来校准模型偏好，并在推理时提供上下文管理策略来平衡探索与利用。

<h3 id="608">[608] AgentArk: Distilling Multi-Agent Intelligence into a Single LLM Agent</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了AgentArk框架，通过层次化蒸馏策略将多代理动态蒸馏到单一模型权重中。该方法将显式的测试时交互转化为隐式模型能力，使单一代理在保持计算效率的同时具备多代理系统的推理和自纠错能力。

<h3 id="609">[609] Position: Universal Time Series Foundation Models Rest on a Category Error</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.05287](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05287) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xilin Dai, Wanxu Cai, Zhijian Xu, Qiang Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Foundation Model, Causal Inference
- **Summary**: 本文认为"通用时间序列基础模型"存在根本性的范畴错误，将结构容器误认为语义模态。作者提出"自回归盲区界限"理论，证明仅依赖历史的模型无法预测干预驱动的状态转变，并主张用因果控制智能体范式替代通用模型。

<h3 id="610">[610] RaBiT: Residual-Aware Binarization Training for Accurate and Efficient LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.05367](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05367) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Quantization, LLM Inference
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出RaBiT量化框架，通过从单一共享全精度权重顺序推导每个二进制路径来解决残差二值化中的路径间适应问题。该方法在2位精度下达到最优性能，在RTX 4090上实现4.49倍推理加速。

<h3 id="611">[611] Does Your Reasoning Model Implicitly Know When to Stop Thinking?</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI

- **Summary**: 研究发现大型推理模型隐式知道何时停止思考，但这一能力被当前采样范式所掩盖。作者提出SAGE采样范式及其强化学习变体SAGE-RL，有效提升推理准确性和效率。

<h3 id="612">[612] Conv-FinRe: A Conversational and Longitudinal Benchmark for Utility-Grounded Financial Recommendation</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出Token Games评估框架，让模型通过互相设计编程谜题进行对决，利用Elo评级比较模型推理能力。该方法以低于200美元成本复现了现有基准的模型排名。

<h3 id="615">[615] LiTS: A Modular Framework for LLM Tree Search</h3>
